# KTM 450 XC-F 2020: aftermarket parts

5026 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 454 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1853 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2719 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
